description: "Complete outgoing Session V4 persistence inventory captured before the V5 writer change."

# Session V4 persistence format

English | [中文](v4.zh.md)

## Summary

This archive captures the uncommitted, unaccepted local working tree immediately before the V5 writer change. It includes the temporary V4 official expert/RAG records already written by that tree. It is not a published tag, accepted compatibility checkpoint, or pull request.

## Source evidence

`verify-persistence-catalog` passed while the writer was V4; `verify-persistence-formats --archive 4` then captured this complete inventory before the writer changed. `workingTreeSnapshot` is the SHA-256 of the exact archived schema bytes, checked by the format verifier. It identifies only this local schema capture; publication remains owned by [version/status](../../session-format-status.md).

## Format characteristics

V4 uses tool-role result messages, explicit surface placement, embedded Assistant streams, and inherited end-seed markers. Source kinds, optional text reference metadata and opaque plugin payloads remain unchanged. The [retained V4 codec](../../../packages/session/session-format-v3-to-v4/src/codec.ts) owns physical framing. The [V4→V5 library](../../../packages/session/session-format-v4-to-v5/README.md) owns outgoing admission and conversion.

## Declaration

[Schema inventory](v4.schema.json).

## Verification and limitations

The inventory describes declarations in the local capture. It does not establish that old V4 recovery safely refuses new required events after an earlier corrupt row. V5 supplies a header discriminator before recovery. Existing finalized and released records remain unchanged.
